    12→<script type="text/javascript">
    13→  const openDialog = (dialogId, event) => {
    14→    let dialog = document.getElementById(dialogId);
    15→    const dialogContainer = document.getElementById(
    16→      'template-modal-container',
    17→    );
    18→
    19→    if (!dialog) {
    20→      const dialogContent = dialogContainer.content.cloneNode(true);
    21→
    22→      dialog = dialogContent.querySelector(
    23→        `#${dialogId}`,
    24→      );
    25→
    26→      if (!dialog) {
    27→        throw Error(`No such dialog with id: ${dialogId} exists!`);
    28→      }
    29→
    30→      dialogContainer.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', dialog);
    31→    }
    32→
    33→    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    34→      dialog.show();
    35→
    36→      if (event?.target) {
    37→        requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    38→          event.target.closest('.is-loading')?.classList.remove('is-loading');
    39→        });
    40→      }
    41→    });
    42→
    43→    return dialog
    44→  };
    45→
    46→  window.openDialog = openDialog
    47→</script>
